### R05-R04 implementation, focused verification, and local Release update — 2026-10-10

- **Implementation decisions:** Runtime Controls, labels, panels, preview and grid now use the original 1536×1024 design coordinate system with a uniform fit scale and centered offset derived from the current client size. This preserves the three-master proportions and maps button visuals and hitboxes together. The preview retains nearest-neighbor filtering and its zoom factor. Both single and multi file-selection callbacks share one `batch-import` dispatch. Import clears stale selected-source/pipeline/preview state, then uses only per-item `disposition`, `source_id`, and `error` from the canonical batch contract; it previews the first successful item, identifies each selected filename and identity, distinguishes `ALREADY_IMPORTED`, and reports rejected files with reasons. Selection does not dispatch `pipeline`; the existing Run Pipeline control remains the explicit action. The PNG chooser no longer offers CSV, and the CSV generation action is separately tagged to retain its explicit Run Batch behavior. The Godot 4.7 runtime lacked the existing `Image.get_used_colors()` call, so displayed color count now uses a pixel-level RGBA set.
- **Failed attempts and corrections:** The first invocation through the GUI-subsystem Godot executable emitted only its engine banner and no test marker/usable exit status; switched to `Godot_v4.7.2-stable_win64_console.exe`. The first console test parse failed because the test called a non-static GDScript method on the script resource; changed the geometry helper call to use an instance. A scene-based attempt found the source project's imported `.godot` texture cache absent and exposed `Image.get_used_colors()` failing at runtime; the bounded UI test now instantiates the controller with only its canvas and FileDialog controls and uses existing repository PNG files, avoiding an editor import. Test diagnostics also found assertion-formatting mistakes and that the initial test double used `state` while the actual batch importer returns `disposition`; the test double/assertions were corrected to match `src/scrubbots_pixel_factory/studio_extensions.py`. A first Release-close command used PowerShell's read-only `$PID` variable by mistake and failed before stopping any process; a fresh identity query then found the verified Release Studio PID had already exited. No process was terminated.
- **Changed focused test:** `level_factory/tests/factory_studio_r04_responsive_png_intake_suite.gd` exercises the live controller's Select PNG hitbox at 920×610 and 1920×1080, the 1536×1024 reference geometry, one selection, three distinct existing valid tiny fixture paths (20×20, 20×21, 32×32), same-file `ALREADY_IMPORTED` reporting, the existing 1536×1024 Level Factory master rejection, preview source identity, and the separation between selection dispatches and one explicit pipeline dispatch. Run/resume rejects a mismatched persisted plan before dispatch. Retry creation verifies current PNG and source-record hashes, immutable composite membership, exact parent chain, and retained source column assignment. For an exact legacy composite, the backend writes a separate immutable `EXPLICIT_RESUME` reconciliation record only when the owner explicitly calls Run/Resume; lookup and startup remain read-only.
- Changed `level_factory/scripts/factory_core_launcher.py` to route `loaded-png-batch-find` to the read-only resolver.
- Changed `level_factory/scripts/factory_studio_exact_ui.gd`: startup rebinds the current ordered gameplay gallery to its exact composite/job using read-only operations; PAUSED/STOPPED/QUEUED jobs show explicit Resume, persisted RUNNING is presented as local INTERRUPTED with no active worker, and COMPLETE results show truthful READY/failure counts. A verified retry child can expose another `Retry Failed` even when its item count is below nine. Retry controls require backend `lineage_verified` plus source and source-record SHA agreement. Resume dispatches the same persisted job; no composite/job is generated during restore or thumbnail selection. Legacy unique matches display a separate explicit “Review & Resume Legacy Batch” action; ambiguous authority disables resume/retry.
- Same-source duplicate-import decision: an additional v1 import event is retained only as provenance on a newly created immutable snapshot; the content identity and existing job are independent of later duplicate import events. The provenance digest still detects tampering. No game repository, solver, replay, load-check, Release Pool result, R2, STAGING or PRODUCTION behavior was authorized or modified.

## R09 implementation, focused evidence, and Release installation

- **Diagnosis:** `_select_imported_source_by_id` previously re-rendered any prior `_batch_snapshot` with a source count and assigned its completed-job counts to Activity, even when that nine-source job did not match the currently loaded 140-source library. `_record_verified_batch_item_progress` appended every completed item into the shared Activity list, so switching the active item could leave rows for a prior source. The old `RUNNING` follow token also omitted job state, so PAUSED/INTERRUPTED/RESUME changes could be hidden when the item token itself was unchanged.
- **Implementation:** Activity selection now clears only its in-memory visual rows/events and stale single-run view state. Exact read-only source-run restore remains source-bound and can show only that PNG's own READY proof. Prior batch results render only when the job's composite exactly matches the full loaded gallery and the selected item identity; batch rows are limited to that current source and READY requires the matching restored run ID. Batch-follow source changes clear prior-source Activity rows and reset the stream/elapsed state. The follow token includes job state, and the header follows PAUSED/INTERRUPTED/RESUME. Mismatched historic jobs are not polled into the current source view; the persisted snapshot stays unchanged, and the explicit `Run Entire Loaded Batch (140)` control remains available and was not clicked. The five-card layout, Release Pool data, launcher source, and publication gates were not reworked.
- **Focused test added:** `level_factory/tests/factory_studio_r09_r02_r04_r09_source_activity_scope_suite.gd`. The mock covers 140 loaded sources with a prior nine-item COMPLETE/9 READY job; selection 011 SOURCE_ONLY, verified source-only 009, unsolved 012 then 011, tab switching, centered five cards, retained nine Pool entries, and availability (without activation) of the explicit 140 action. Matching 9-item and 140-item RUNNING fixtures follow 005→006, clear prior-source rows, then exercise PAUSED, INTERRUPTED, RESUME, and completed 006 READY. A gateway spy confirms no batch creation/run, solver, replay, load-check, STAGING/PRODUCTION call on navigation or rendering. Persisted mock snapshots and Pool values remain unchanged.
- **Pre-fix RED:** with the source-under-test before controller edits, the corrected suite failed for the exact owner defect: 011 inherited READY and old 002–010 rows, 009 showed unrelated rows, 012 inherited status, tab switching retained old rows, and completed 005 remained visible after the active source advanced to 006. This established the targeted RED.
- **Test setup and invocation corrections:** first test setup omitted the required `MasterCanvas`, causing a nil Activity label; added the same named canvas used by the existing R08 harness. The first Godot shim version guard stopped before running the suite; a subsequent stdout-capture attempt did not return and left two task-owned Godot test processes (PIDs 16964 and 19196). Their exact R09 test command lines were verified before stopping only those processes. The installed concrete Godot 4.7.2 console executable provided reliable PASS/exit output. During diagnosis, one temporary test iteration directly appended the expected batch row before its assertion and therefore produced an invalid PASS; that instrumentation was removed, and it is not reported as evidence. The first coarse gateway assertion reported a failure without naming the operation; it was tightened to list only the explicitly forbidden operations, and the final run passed that guard with none recorded.

A first read-only output inventory helper called its hash function before declaring it and produced no digest; corrected the function order and computed the preinstall inventory. ## C001-R03 continuation - deterministic CP08 observed-object validation

- **Finding:** `verify_content_health` accepted arbitrary runtime mapping keys despite its string-path contract, then sorted unexpected keys. Mixed key types could raise an incidental `TypeError`; unsafe path keys could be emitted as evidence.

- Source review found a concrete ART_FIRST junction escape: `_candidate_and_review()` rejects `Path.is_symlink()` for `candidate_dir` but does not anchor the resolved `candidate_dir` to the canonical `candidate_store`; `_safe_file()` then treats that same directory as its own containment root. A directory junction can report `is_symlink() == False`, so a junction target outside the reviewed candidate store can satisfy the file-relative check. Separately, `owner_upload_root()` returned `.resolve()` without checking canonical path components first, so a junction on the trusted store path could redirect that root before downstream checks. Current exact project roots are not reparse points; this is a source-level bypass if an attacker/reparse mutation replaces a trust-boundary directory. The explicit `source_directory` override in Studio resolves a caller-selected alternate location and is used by batch/recovery paths; ART_FIRST and production bundle/Pool verification use the default canonical store and do not accept this override. - F01 implementation: `current_game_contract.py` now inventories `level_validator.gd` and checks the pinned level spec / loader markers for V1/V2 VOID, production envelope, production label classes vs TEST, 3..12 used colors, canonical palette, FIFO schema/version, global unique batch identity and conservation. Static inputs reject duplicate JSON keys/non-finite constants, oversized raw LevelData/plan, overlarge snapshots, and encoded source inventories before base64 decode. Static LevelData rejects missing/blank id/name/difficulty, TEST/unknown production label, non-3..12 used colors, and candidate/level mismatch. FIFO batches reject missing, blank, or duplicate `batchId`. Positive V1 and V2 fixtures are 20x20, have all required fields, three canonical used colors, unique IDs and exact totals; V2 retains 200 artwork cells and 200 VOID cells. Launcher request JSON/file/base64 inputs now have a 384 KiB pre-parse/decode bound.
- F02 implementation: selected-source verification still calls the existing `validate_pool_candidate_provenance`. It now requires at least one existing trusted SHA field from the returned verified classification, candidate source lineage, READY owner binding, or READY primary; every supplied authority must be a valid SHA-256 and match actual selected `source.json` bytes. Missing authority returns exact `SOURCE_RECORD_DIGEST_AUTHORITY_MISSING`; disagreement rejects. No cohort enumeration occurred; result surfaces `SAVED_HISTORICAL_UNVERIFIED` and compatibility scope `SELECTED_SOURCE_ONLY`. Focused negatives cover missing/wrong digest, TEST, missing ACCEPT, and mismatched selected grid; the integrated positive exercises actual source PNG decode/grid/classification/review checks on memory-supplied bytes.
- F03 implementation: added pure `describe_current_main_replay_requirements` to the existing CPX002 adapter and used it from Factory bridge. Claims, booleans, fake roots/hashes, mismatched source/game hashes, and a receipt-shaped mapping remain `CLAIMED_TYPED_UNVERIFIED`; descriptor is never verified and the bridge always says `CPX002_AUTHORITY_REQUIRED` / `CURRENT_MAIN_RUNTIME_NOT_EXECUTED`.
